# The Drowned Deep Art Bible

Entries for the locked cut of the Drowned Deep set: the 155 creatures, in
the order of the cut list (rarity, then colour), which is the order the data
file will take when it lands. The **Drowned Deep register** in
[index.md](index.md) section 4c governs every palette, value floor, lighting
pair, species tell, reserved hue and mechanic-family composition below, and
the global style contract in section 2 remains in force above it. The 97
non-creature cards are in [spell-art.md](../spell-art.md) under "Drowned Deep
non-creatures", and the four set tokens are in
[constructs-and-tokens.md](constructs-and-tokens.md).

**Card facts are transcribed from the cut list in
[drowned-deep-overplan.md](../expansions/drafts/drowned-deep-overplan.md)**,
which is the locked design. `src/data/cards/drowned-deep.ts` does not exist
yet; when it lands, `check-art-bible` becomes the authority and every fact
line here must agree with it.

Four register rules bind hardest and are repeated here because they are the
ones a generator breaks first:

- **Drowned gold `#d6e07c` is reserved for the Deep.** Whispers light in the
  water and the light behind a Horror's eyes, lit from below, named by bearer
  and direction. Never lamps, never bells, never fire, never ambience; every
  lamp, bell or flame in the same frame gets its own colour word.
- **The Watch lights from above in lamp amber and carries none of the Deep's
  three tells.** A Warden is never lit from below; a Horror is never lit in
  amber.
- **Water is a surface, not a filter.** A flat reflective plane with a visible
  edge against stone; never a blue wash over the figure.
- **No charts with names, no ship names, no lettered bells.** The sea variant
  of NO-TEXT rides every prompt.

The coast's identity by colour: the Lantern Watch in oilskin, wool and bell
bronze on the granite of the Point and the Salt Stair (white); the sea's
memory, tide-priestesses, Mermaids and drowned scholars with blank vellum on
the Reach (blue); the bargain, salt-witches with sealed jars, the Deep's
daughters and the drowned of Low Street and the Wharf (black); the coral
growth, reef-wardens, Kelp Shades and the kelp cathedral of the Salt Marsh
(green); drowned fire and storm, wreckers and storm-callers on the Breakwater
(red).

## Catalog order

Creature entries follow the cut list order (rarity, then colour). Authoring
waves remain in git history.
### Mother Hydra, Queen Beneath Dunmarrow — `dd-mother-hydra`
- **Card facts:** {5}{B}{B} · B · 6/7 · dreaded · ur, legendary · holo: prism
- **Character & source:** The Deep's queen, an adult woman of vast build, Dunmarrow's landlord for three centuries. Registry-bound: bell-chain crown, her Deep-Spawn brood around her. Pale grey-green skin, ash hair, broad shoulders.
- **Personality / mood:** She has never once raised the rent. Serene, unhurried, certain of what she is owed.
- **Pose & composition:** Wide pulled-back shot, seated on the drowned bell tower's granite parapet, still black water at her waist, hands open on her knees; exactly three small wet-haired Deep-Spawn daughters at her hips. Eye line y 320 in the safe band, head top below the top third. Tithe state: pale given bodies slipping down under the flat surface beneath her, silhouettes only.
- **Costume & attire:** Bronze bell chains crossing her chest as crown and collar, a sodden court gown of mourning violet split to the thigh, weed woven through the silk, bare forearms.
- **Palette:** B anchor `#5a3a70` lifted to wet-stone `#2b3238`; black water `#0d1a22`; bell bronze `#a8783c`; drowned gold `#d6e07c` behind her eyes and in the pool beneath her.
- **Lighting:** Key: drowned gold rising from below the surface beneath her, centred. One mourning-violet `#8b6fb0` rim from the storm sky.
- **Expression:** Faint closed-mouth smile, heavy-lidded gold-lit eyes on the viewer.
- **Props / weapon:** The bell-chain crown; no weapon. Drowned detail: one tide-line across her collarbone.
- **Background:** Dunmarrow at low water: the bell tower beside her, granite roofs under glass-still black water.
- **Holo interaction:** Prism: the centred gold pool beneath her is the one light source; face bright, sky quiet.
- **Rarity ambition:** Set-defining box-art splash: the Deep's court in one frame.
- **Prompt:** EXACTLY ONE adult woman, a vast serene Deep One Horror queen, and exactly three small wet-haired Deep-Spawn daughters at her hips, wide pulled-back shot, seated on a drowned granite bell tower parapet, still black water at her waist, her head fully inside the middle band of the frame, clear air above it, a drowned-gold light behind her eyes, hair lifting slowly as if underwater, one tide-line across her collarbone, bronze bell chains as crown and collar, sodden mourning violet gown, the daughters with the same gold behind the eyes, pale given bodies slipping down under the flat surface beneath her, silhouettes only, key drowned gold #d6e07c behind her eyes and rising from below the surface beneath her, one cool rim mourning violet #8b6fb0 from a storm sky, bells bronze, no cyan anywhere in the frame, no true black, darkest visible value wet-stone grey #2b3238, water drawn as a flat reflective surface with a visible edge against stone, fully rendered background with a hard granite, bronze or timber edge in it, drowned roofs under the water, no gore, no rot, no exposed bone, no tentacles, no helmets, no visors, no diving suits, the sky overcast grey with no sun, no sunset, no sunrise and no golden sky, no gold jewellery, no gold trim on cloth, every chain, crown and fitting bell bronze or tarnished steel, no text, no charts with names, no ship names, no lettered bells, no lettered lamps, no gravestone inscriptions, no signboards, no nameplates, no numerals, no writing on any surface — crisp cel-shaded gacha anime splash art, fully rendered scenic background, 640×800 portrait

### Dagon, the Deep Itself — `dd-father-dagon`
- **Card facts:** {6}{U}{B} · U/B (gold frame) · 8/8 · dreaded, overrun · ur, legendary · holo: shiny
- **Character & source:** Dagon, the oldest office on the coast, held by an adult woman: the Deep given a body. Registry-bound: Dunmarrow's bell tower in her frame. Colossal, grey-blue skin, white hair lifting as if underwater. Gold frame.
- **Personality / mood:** The harbour floor is not the bottom. It is her brow. Immense, patient, older than the town.
- **Pose & composition:** Extreme wide establishing shot, crown to waterline, nothing cropped: shoulders and head above the harbour beside the bell tower, which reaches her chin, hands flat on the water either side. Eye line y 320 in the safe band, head top below the top third. Tithe state: pale given bodies slipping under the flat surface beneath her, silhouettes only; three blank pages settle beside them (each player grinds).
- **Costume & attire:** Bronze bell chains across her chest, a sodden mantle of mourning violet and deep blue, weed through the hair.
- **Palette:** U `#16294f`, black water `#0d1a22`, bell bronze `#a8783c`, salt white `#eef0ea` pages, drowned gold `#d6e07c` behind her eyes and in the water beneath her.
- **Lighting:** Key: drowned gold from below the surface beneath her. One mourning-violet rim from the storm sky.
- **Expression:** Eyes half-closed, a slow serene smile, looking down at the town.
- **Props / weapon:** The bell tower beside her; no weapon.
- **Background:** Dunmarrow at low water: the bell tower breaking the surface, drowned granite roofs.
- **Holo interaction:** Shiny: sky quiet, face bright; water carries the shimmer.
- **Rarity ambition:** Set-defining box-art splash: the Deep with a face.

### Maren Holt, the Lightkeeper — `dd-lightkeeper`
- **Card facts:** {4}{W}{W} · W · 3/6 · sentinel, wardingGaze · ur, legendary · holo: prism
- **Character & source:** Maren Holt, an adult woman in her forties, Lightkeeper of the Point. Broad-shouldered, wind-burned, iron-grey hair cut blunt at the jaw. Registry canon: the great lamp is her secondary element.
- **Personality / mood:** The light has not gone dark in three hundred years, and she is why. Unhurried and absolute.
- **Pose & composition:** Waist-up, three-quarter left, planted in the lantern room, both hands on the great lamp's bronze crank mid-turn (Duty); Sentinel stands, eyes on the viewer for Warding Gaze. The lens sits at frame centre behind her. Eye line y 340 inside the safe band y 200 to 560, head top below the top third.
- **Costume & attire:** Heavy tan oilskin coat over a grey wool waistcoat and cream salt-stiffened linen, bronze lamp-key ring at the belt, bare forearms. No tells.
- **Palette:** W `#f2e8cf` / `#c9a84c`, bell bronze `#a8783c` housing, lamp amber `#ffb35c` / `#ffd08a` dominant, mourning violet `#5a3f7a` beyond the glass.
- **Lighting:** Practical key: the great lamp behind her, lamp amber from above onto her face, the warmest thing in the frame. One cool rim in mourning violet from the storm sky.
- **Expression:** Level, dry, faintly amused.
- **Props / weapon:** The great lamp of the Point, a bronze-housed lens taller than she is. No weapon.
- **Background:** The lantern room atop the lighthouse on the Point: bronze-framed panes, a granite sill, the Reach far below.
- **Holo interaction:** Prism: the lens is the single centred light source; her face stays off the centre line.
- **Rarity ambition:** UR legendary hero splash: the woman, the lamp, the coast beneath.

### Isolde Marrow, Drowned Cartographer — `dd-isolde-marrow`
- **Card facts:** {2}{U}{U} · U · 2/5 · ur, legendary · holo: prism
- **Character & source:** The Drowned Cartographer of the canon registry: an adult woman, narrow-shouldered, cool pale skin, long ash-dark hair, ink-stained cuffs, blank vellum charts. Drowned, still working; she carries the three tells.
- **Personality / mood:** Her charts are accurate to the inch, for a coast that no longer exists. Exact, unhurried, amused that anyone still asks the way.
- **Pose & composition:** Three-quarter, thigh-up, kneeling on the lowest wet step of the Salt Stair, one hand lifting a dripping blank chart from flat black water (the Duty, mid-act), the other holding a pen. Eye line y 340, head top below the top third; the lamp centred above her.
- **Costume & attire:** Long deep-blue scholar's coat over a fitted linen bodice, sleeves rolled, chart-case on a strap, bare forearms.
- **Palette:** U anchor `#4a90d9` to `#16294f`; black water `#0d1a22`; salt white `#eef0ea` vellum; lamp amber `#ffb35c`; granite `#7d8590`. Drowned gold `#d6e07c` behind her eyes only.
- **Lighting:** Practical key: one bronze harbour lamp in lamp amber hanging centred above her. One mourning violet `#5a3f7a` rim from the storm sky.
- **Expression:** Eyes down on the chart, a small dry half-smile, the gold behind them faint.
- **Props / weapon:** The blank chart, the pen, the chart-case. No weapon.
- **Background:** The Salt Stair, granite steps into the harbour; Dunmarrow's bell tower breaks the surface far out, bronze bell dark.
- **Holo interaction:** Prism: the centred amber lamp is the single source the rings breathe from; face bright, water textured.
- **Rarity ambition:** UR legendary hero splash: the cartographer at work, the drowned town in frame.
- **Prompt:** EXACTLY ONE adult woman, narrow-shouldered, cool pale skin, long ash-dark hair, kneeling on the lowest wet granite step of a harbour stair, a dripping blank vellum chart held flat in both hands at chest height with its top edge toward the viewer, water running off its lower edge into the flat black water, a pen tucked behind her ear, exactly two arms and exactly two hands with five fingers each, every hand fully accounted for,  her head fully inside the middle band of the frame, clear air above it, a faint drowned-gold light behind her eyes, hair lifting slowly as if underwater, one tide-line across her collarbone, ink-stained cuffs, long deep-blue scholar's coat over a fitted linen bodice, bare forearms, one bronze harbour lamp in lamp amber hanging centred directly above her as the key, one mourning violet rim from a storm sky, drowned gold #d6e07c behind her eyes as if from below the surface, nowhere else in the frame, lamp amber, distant bell bronze, a drowned bell tower far out, no cyan anywhere in the frame, no true black, darkest visible value wet-stone grey #2b3238, water drawn as a flat reflective surface with a visible edge against stone, fully rendered background with a hard granite or bronze edge in it, never a blue colour wash, no helmets, no visors, no diving suits, the sky overcast grey with no sun, no sunset, no sunrise and no golden sky, no gold jewellery, no gold trim on cloth, every chain, crown and fitting bell bronze or tarnished steel, no text, no charts with names, no ship names, no lettered bells, no lettered lamps, no gravestone inscriptions, no signboards, no nameplates, no numerals, no writing on any surface — crisp cel-shaded gacha anime splash art, fully rendered scenic background, 640×800 portrait

### The Brood Below — `dd-the-brood-below`
- **Card facts:** {5}{B}{B} · B · 5/5 · ur · holo: shiny
- **Character & source:** An adult woman, a broad-shouldered Deep One Horror brood-mother risen among the spring nets off Low Street. Grey-green skin, dark hair to the waist. Registry-bound: her two Deep-Spawn match the token canon.
- **Personality / mood:** What the nets bring up in spring is not fish, and the town has learned not to count. Calm, proprietary, faintly pleased.
- **Pose & composition:** Wide shot, chest-deep in the still black harbour among slack nets, one hand on each daughter's head; exactly two small wet-haired Deep-Spawn daughters at her sides. Eye line y 320 in the safe band, head top below the top third. Tithe state: pale given bodies slipping down under the flat surface beneath her, silhouettes only.
- **Costume & attire:** A torn net worn as a shawl over a sodden grey-green bodice, glass net-floats knotted in her hair, bare forearms.
- **Palette:** B anchor `#5a3a70` to wet-stone `#2b3238`, black water `#0d1a22`, salt white `#eef0ea` foam and float glass, drowned gold `#d6e07c` behind all three pairs of eyes and rising in the water beneath her.
- **Lighting:** Key: drowned gold from below the surface beneath the three of them. One mourning-violet rim from the overcast above the wharf.
- **Expression:** Half-lidded, a slow closed smile, chin lowered toward her daughters.
- **Props / weapon:** The nets and the glass floats; no weapon.
- **Background:** Low Street wharf at spring tide: timber pilings, a granite quay edge, moored hulls behind.
- **Holo interaction:** Shiny: light shared across three bearers, no single centre; faces bright, water textured.
- **Rarity ambition:** Gold-tier splash with the canon secondary element: the brood.

### The Reef That Walks — `dd-the-reef-that-walks`
- **Card facts:** {6}{G}{G} · G · 7/8 · overrun · ur · holo: prism
- **Character & source:** A Deep One Horror that grew a reef and got up: an adult woman of vast build, coral plates over grey-green skin, kelp for hair. Three tells.
- **Personality / mood:** "Coral grows on whatever stands still long enough. The reef stopped standing still." Immense, patient appetite.
- **Pose & composition:** Wide shot, she wades out of the marsh toward camera, eye line y 340 inside the safe band y 200 to 560, head top below the top third. Two Kelp Shades flank her, each already carrying exactly one cyan coral bloom at mid-height on the body, fully visible in the upper two thirds of the frame, never near the bottom edge. Beneath her two given figures slip under the flat surface.
- **Costume & attire:** No cloth; coral plates over shoulders, chest and hips, a kelp skirt, one tide-line of salt crust across her collarbones.
- **Palette:** Kelp green `#3f6b4a`, black water `#0d1a22`, drowned gold `#d6e07c` behind her eyes and below her, cyan `#5ff0e0` on the two Shades only; cyan never touches her.
- **Lighting:** Key: drowned gold from below the surface, centred beneath her. Rim: mourning violet `#5a3f7a` from storm cloud.
- **Expression:** Serene, mouth closed, gold behind the eyes unmistakable.
- **Props / weapon:** None; her reach is the weapon.
- **Background:** The Salt Marsh at night, kelp cathedral columns behind her, a granite breakwater at frame right.
- **Holo interaction:** Prism, rewarded by the centred gold pool beneath her; face bright, coral textured.
- **Rarity ambition:** UR box-art splash: the reef walking, Marks lit on her escort.

### Old Marrow, Keeper of the Salt Marsh — `dd-old-marrow`
- **Card facts:** {3}{G}{G} · G · 4/6 · bulwark · ur, legendary · holo: shiny
- **Character & source:** Old Marrow from the canon registry, keeper of the Salt Marsh: an adult woman, tall and weathered, warm brown skin lined by salt wind, iron-grey hair in one heavy braid. The coast's: no tells.
- **Personality / mood:** "She plants the drowned where the reeds are thickest, and something always comes up." A patient gardener of the dead, unhurried and immovable.
- **Pose & composition:** Duty mid-work: knee-deep in reeds, both hands pressing a reed-stake into the mud, feet set, Bulwark planted. One Kelp Shade stands half-risen beside her, kelp streaming. Three-quarter, eye line y 330 inside the safe band y 200 to 560, head top below the top third.
- **Costume & attire:** Kelp-dyed green wool coat over a fitted bodice, layered shawls, coral pieces on a leather thong at her throat, sleeves pushed up, bare forearms.
- **Palette:** Kelp green `#3f6b4a` / `#7fb07a`, marsh water `#16303a`, salt-white shawl `#eef0ea`, lamp amber `#ffb35c`. No cyan, no drowned gold.
- **Lighting:** Key: a marsh lantern in lamp amber hung from a pole above her left shoulder, lighting her from above. Rim: mourning violet `#5a3f7a` from the dusk behind.
- **Expression:** A dry half-smile, eyes on the rising Shade, unsurprised.
- **Props / weapon:** The reed-stake, the lantern pole, the coral thong.
- **Background:** The Salt Marsh at dusk, reed beds, one kelp cathedral column in flat black water, a timber post at frame right.
- **Holo interaction:** Shiny; the lantern is off-centre, so keep the amber field textured.
- **Rarity ambition:** UR legendary hero splash with the canon secondary element, the Kelp Shade.
- **Prompt:** EXACTLY ONE adult woman, a tall weathered legendary marsh witch knee-deep in reeds pressing a long reed-stake into the mud with both hands, and EXACTLY ONE woman-shaped figure of woven kelp half-risen from the reeds beside her, two pale eyes only, her head fully inside the middle band of the frame, clear air above it, iron-grey hair in one heavy braid, warm brown lined skin, no gold behind the eyes, kelp-dyed green wool coat over a fitted bodice with layered shawls, coral on a thong, bare forearms, no cyan anywhere in the frame, no drowned gold anywhere in the frame, a marsh lantern in lamp amber #ffb35c on a pole above her left shoulder as the only key, one cool rim in mourning violet #5a3f7a from the dusk, dry half-smile, eyes on the kelp figure, dusk reed beds, one kelp cathedral column in flat black water, timber post at right, no true black, darkest visible value wet-stone grey #2b3238, water drawn as a flat reflective surface with a visible edge, fully rendered background with a hard granite, coral or timber edge in it, no helmets, no visors, no diving suits, the sky overcast grey with no sun, no sunset, no sunrise and no golden sky, no gold jewellery, no gold trim on cloth, every chain, crown and fitting bell bronze or tarnished steel, no text, no charts with names, no ship names, no lettered bells, no lettered lamps, no gravestone inscriptions, no signboards, no nameplates, no numerals, no writing on any surface — crisp cel-shaded gacha anime splash art, fully rendered scenic background, 640×800 portrait

### Brenna Gale, Storm-Caller of the Reach — `dd-brenna-gale`
